# Vegetable juices root vegetable containing

<section class="hmi-quick-read" id="vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing-quick-read" data-hmi-quick-read aria-labelledby="vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing-quick-read-label"><div class="hmi-quick-read-label" id="vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing-quick-read-label">Quick read</div>

Vegetable juices root vegetable containing in the HMI library has 102 reported results for lead from 1 source.<sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2018</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Analytical Results for Lead in Juice Sampled Under the FDA&#39;s Toxic Elements in Food and Foodware, and Radionuclides in Food – Import and Domestic Compliance Program (FY2005-FY2018)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA analytical results table</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

<ul>
<li>
<strong>Metals measured:</strong>

Lead, in 102 results from 1 source.<sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2018</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Analytical Results for Lead in Juice Sampled Under the FDA&#39;s Toxic Elements in Food and Foodware, and Radionuclides in Food – Import and Domestic Compliance Program (FY2005-FY2018)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA analytical results table</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>
<li>
<strong>Main drivers:</strong>

Form, basis and where the vegetable juices root vegetable containing was sampled; reporting limits decide how many results are non-detects.<sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2018</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Analytical Results for Lead in Juice Sampled Under the FDA&#39;s Toxic Elements in Food and Foodware, and Radionuclides in Food – Import and Domestic Compliance Program (FY2005-FY2018)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA analytical results table</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>
<li>
<strong>Practical framing:</strong>

Ask for lot results with a quantification limit well below the limit that applies in the destination market.<sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2018</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Analytical Results for Lead in Juice Sampled Under the FDA&#39;s Toxic Elements in Food and Foodware, and Radionuclides in Food – Import and Domestic Compliance Program (FY2005-FY2018)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA analytical results table</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>
</ul>
</section>

<a id="product-definition-and-scope"></a>
## What counts as vegetable juices root vegetable containing

<a id="hmi-prd-001"></a>
<a id="what-finished-product-category-formulations-formats-and-intended-uses-does-this-page-cover"></a>
<a id="hmi-prd-002"></a>
<a id="which-subcategories-or-preparation-states-require-separate-evidence-and-regulatory-comparisons"></a>
<a id="hmi-prd-003"></a>
<a id="which-populations-and-exposure-routes-are-relevant-to-this-product"></a>

This page covers vegetable juices root vegetable containing, juice (102 results).<sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2018</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Analytical Results for Lead in Juice Sampled Under the FDA&#39;s Toxic Elements in Food and Foodware, and Radionuclides in Food – Import and Domestic Compliance Program (FY2005-FY2018)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA analytical results table</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

Results stay on the basis each source reports: as sold or fresh weight, dry weight, or unstated. Dry-weight, as-sold and unstated results are never pooled, and no moisture conversion is applied.

## Vegetable juices root vegetable containing overview

<a id="which-uncertainties-prevent-a-reliable-conclusion-about-the-category-or-a-particular-product"></a>

Vegetable juices root vegetable containing in the HMI library has 102 reported results for lead from 1 source.<sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2018</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Analytical Results for Lead in Juice Sampled Under the FDA&#39;s Toxic Elements in Food and Foodware, and Radionuclides in Food – Import and Domestic Compliance Program (FY2005-FY2018)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA analytical results table</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>



For a buyer the practical points are the form bought, the basis a result is reported on, where the vegetable juices root vegetable containing came from and the laboratory’s reporting limit. Testing finds contamination but does not remove it.

<a id="literature-evidence-summary"></a>
## Heavy metal contamination profile of vegetable juices root vegetable containing

<a id="hmi-prd-007"></a>
<a id="what-has-been-measured-directly-in-finished-products-of-the-exact-formulation-and-format"></a>
<a id="hmi-prd-008"></a>
<a id="what-concentrations-detection-frequencies-and-variability-were-reported-in-defined-markets-and-periods"></a>
<a id="hmi-prd-009"></a>
<a id="how-representative-are-the-observations-and-which-related-product-findings-provide-context-only"></a>

The table holds every extracted vegetable juices root vegetable containing result: one row for each statistic a source reports for a form, group and metal, 102 rows in all, grouped by metal and form; percentiles, ranges and maxima for the same samples as a mean or median are shown on that row.<sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2018</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Analytical Results for Lead in Juice Sampled Under the FDA&#39;s Toxic Elements in Food and Foodware, and Radionuclides in Food – Import and Domestic Compliance Program (FY2005-FY2018)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA analytical results table</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> Monitoring medians are HMI calculations from individual submissions; non-detects remain intervals between zero and the reporting limit and are never read as zero.

47 rows can be rated against an HMI reference for the same form and basis, 0 rows have a direct same-study comparison, and the rest stay unrated with the reason recorded.

<div class="hmi-analyte-explorer" id="hmi-occurrence-vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing" data-analyte-explorer data-compact-intro="true" data-source-summaries="true"><details class="hmi-drawer-disclosure" data-explorer-context><summary data-open-context aria-haspopup="dialog" aria-controls="hmi-occurrence-vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing-drawer">Comparison methods and scope</summary><div data-explorer-context-content>

<p>Each row is one statistic a source reports for one form of vegetable juices root vegetable containing, group and metal; percentiles, ranges and maxima reported for the same samples as a mean or median are shown on that row. Different forms and preparation states are kept apart, and dry-weight results never share a reference with as-sold or fresh-weight results. Ratings place a result against an HMI reference built from monitoring results and ordinary-setting medians for the same form and basis. Same-study contrasts compare a contaminated or treated site with its control, targeted with random sampling, a prepared form with the unprocessed one, or one group of a study with the median of its other groups. Held rows failed a check against their own source and are shown but not compared.</p>

No matched reference or pooled distribution is inferred.</p></div></details><details class="hmi-drawer-disclosure" data-baseline-fallback id="hmi-reference-hmi-pooled-reference-v2:vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing-d4e6da82eddd:ee96fa527582"><summary>HMI vegetable juices root vegetable containing Pb reference · Vegetable juices root vegetable containing, juice</summary><div data-baseline-reference-template="hmi-pooled-reference-v2:vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing-d4e6da82eddd:ee96fa527582"><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Reference and scope</p><p>HMI vegetable juices root vegetable containing Pb reference · Vegetable juices root vegetable containing, juice. Vegetable juices root vegetable containing, juice; As sold, as eaten or fresh weight, µg/kg; no moisture conversion. Distribution of subgroup median concentration values.</p><div class="hmi-drawer-table"><table><thead><tr><th scope="col">Reference (µg/kg)</th><th scope="col">Q1</th><th scope="col">Median</th><th scope="col">Q3</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Primary HMI reference</td><td>1</td><td>2.5</td><td>10</td></tr><tr><td>Alternative weighting</td><td>1.3</td><td>3</td><td>10</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p>47 reference units; 1 sampling family. Counts describe evidence units, not independent studies or certainty.</p><div class="hmi-drawer-table"><table><thead><tr><th scope="col">Contributing family</th><th scope="col">Reference units</th><th scope="col">Weight</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>U.S. Food and Drug Administration 2018</td><td>47</td><td>100%</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Methods</p><p>HMI pools subgroup medians for vegetable juices root vegetable containing, juice on a as sold, as eaten or fresh weight basis: monitoring results summarized as one median per reporting region, sampling type and collection year, total diet composites and printed sample values as single observations, and printed subgroup medians from ordinary farms or markets. Each sampling family has equal total weight. Non-detects stay as zero-to-limit intervals, so quartiles carry lower and upper bounds. Means, ranges and maxima never enter as observations. Other forms, other bases, targeted sampling and results from mining, industrial, wastewater-affected, experimental, fertiliser or manufacturer settings are kept out of this reference.</p><p>Inverse weighted empirical CDF; midpoint at exact cumulative-weight boundaries. At least four reference units and a definitely positive Q1 to Q3 spread are required. This is a display eligibility rule, not statistical validation.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Limitations</p><p>U.S. Food and Drug Administration 2018 is the only compatible sampling family for vegetable juices root vegetable containing, juice on this basis in the completed vegetable juices root vegetable containing ledger, so this single-family reference cannot represent other markets. Reporting regions and purchase markets are not growing areas. Non-detects with differing limits widen the bounds. A position is descriptive concentration, not a safety or compliance judgment.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Admitted source families</p><ul><li><a href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018" class="internal" data-slug="sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018" data-no-cite-meta>U.S. Food and Drug Administration 2018</a></li></ul><details class="hmi-drawer-disclosure"><summary>Reference version and interpretation</summary><p>Frozen evidence selection: 2026-10-22. Method: hmi-pooled-reference-v2.</p><p>hmi-pooled-reference-v2:vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing-d4e6da82eddd:ee96fa527582</p><p>The reference is computed by HMI from admitted evidence, not copied from a study baseline. It describes the evidence currently in this scope; it is not a worldwide or current-lot benchmark. Source, year and region filters do not change it. Different HMI scopes are not one common ranking.</p></details></div></details><div hidden data-form-reasons><p data-reason="no_numeric_value_or_bound" data-title="Spread or extreme value only">The source reports only a percentile, range or maximum for these samples, with no mean or median to place against the reference. Administration 2018</a></p><p>Vegetable juices root vegetable containing, juice · Lead (Pb) · 1 samples.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Selected finding and reference</p><div class="hmi-selected-result" data-selected-finding><strong class="hmi-finding">4 µg/kg (sample value)</strong><span class="hmi-row-note">sample value.</span><p>1 samples. Basis: As sold, as eaten or fresh weight.</p><p>No censoring reported</p></div><div class="hmi-selected-comparison" data-selected-comparison><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">HMI baseline position</p><p>Moderate · HMI vegetable juices root vegetable containing Pb reference · Vegetable juices root vegetable containing, juice. sample value as reported: 4 µg/kg. HMI median 2.5; Q1 1; Q3 10 µg/kg. 1 family. Not a safety grade.</p><p>Low is below Q1; Moderate includes Q1 through Q3; High is above Q3. Reporting bounds must give the same category throughout.</p><p>This finding’s sampling family contributes to the fixed reference.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sensitivity to weighting</p><p>Equal weight per subgroup: Moderate. The position is unchanged under this alternative. This check does not address all sampling, rounding or analytical uncertainty.</p><div data-baseline-reference="hmi-pooled-reference-v2:vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing-d4e6da82eddd:ee96fa527582"><a href="#hmi-reference-hmi-pooled-reference-v2:vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing-d4e6da82eddd:ee96fa527582">Reference, contributing evidence and methods</a></div></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Collection period</p><p data-timeline-scope>2005–2018</p><div data-selected-timeline><p>No year-by-year sample register is reconstructed from this summary.</p></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Geographic scope</p><p data-regional-scope>FY2009</p><div data-selected-regional><p>Collection year within one study.</p></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sampling, analysis and reporting limits</p><p>FDA elemental analysis method (fda.gov/media/87509/download).</p><p>No censoring reported</p><p>Single-strength (ready to drink) basis; concentrates converted by FDA. Fiscal year is the sampling year per table; FDA sample ID 485188.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Shared samples and study overlap</p><p>Subgroup rows from the same source share its laboratory and sampling frame; they are not independent studies.</p><p>Each table row describes a food/analyte result. Basis: As sold, as eaten or fresh weight.</p><p>No censoring reported</p></div><div class="hmi-selected-comparison" data-selected-comparison><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">HMI baseline position</p><p>High · HMI vegetable juices root vegetable containing Pb reference · Vegetable juices root vegetable containing, juice. sample value as reported: 79.4 µg/kg. HMI median 2.5; Q1 1; Q3 10 µg/kg. 1 family. Not a safety grade.</p><p>Low is below Q1; Moderate includes Q1 through Q3; High is above Q3. Reporting bounds must give the same category throughout.</p><p>This finding’s sampling family contributes to the fixed reference.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sensitivity to weighting</p><p>Equal weight per subgroup: High. The position is unchanged under this alternative. This check does not address all sampling, rounding or analytical uncertainty.</p><div data-baseline-reference="hmi-pooled-reference-v2:vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing-d4e6da82eddd:ee96fa527582"><a href="#hmi-reference-hmi-pooled-reference-v2:vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing-d4e6da82eddd:ee96fa527582">Reference, contributing evidence and methods</a></div></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Collection period</p><p data-timeline-scope>2005–2018</p><div data-selected-timeline><p>No year-by-year sample register is reconstructed from this summary.</p></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Geographic scope</p><p data-regional-scope>FY2018</p><div data-selected-regional><p>Collection year within one study.</p></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sampling, analysis and reporting limits</p><p>FDA elemental analysis method (fda.gov/media/87509/download).</p><p>No censoring reported</p><p>Single-strength (ready to drink) basis; concentrates converted by FDA. Fiscal year is the sampling year per table; FDA sample ID 1041164.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Shared samples and study overlap</p><p>Subgroup rows from the same source share its laboratory and sampling frame; they are not independent studies.</p><p>Each table row describes a food/analyte result. <a id="universal-faqs"></a>
## Vegetable juices root vegetable containing FAQs

<details class="qa" data-hmi-faq="1" id="what-heavy-metals-are-found-in-vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing" data-anchor="what-heavy-metals-are-found-in-vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing" data-question="What heavy metals are found in vegetable juices root vegetable containing?">
<summary><span class="mk" aria-hidden="true"></span>What heavy metals are found in vegetable juices root vegetable containing?</summary>
<div class="ans" data-hmi-faq-answer="1">

The library reports lead in vegetable juices root vegetable containing, most often lead.<sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2018</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Analytical Results for Lead in Juice Sampled Under the FDA&#39;s Toxic Elements in Food and Foodware, and Radionuclides in Food – Import and Domestic Compliance Program (FY2005-FY2018)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA analytical results table</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</div>
</details>

<details class="qa" data-hmi-faq="1" id="which-origins-or-products-of-vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing-measured-lowest" data-anchor="which-origins-or-products-of-vegetable-juices-root-vegetable-containing-measured-lowest" data-question="Which origins or products of vegetable juices root vegetable containing measured lowest?">
<summary><span class="mk" aria-hidden="true"></span>Which origins or products of vegetable juices root vegetable containing measured lowest?</summary>
<div class="ans" data-hmi-faq-answer="1">

In one study, lead across 47 groups of vegetable juices root vegetable containing ranged from 0.3 µg/kg (FY2015) to 79.4 µg/kg (FY2018); the lowest is the lowest observed in that study, not an established sourcing preference.<sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2018</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Analytical Results for Lead in Juice Sampled Under the FDA&#39;s Toxic Elements in Food and Foodware, and Radionuclides in Food – Import and Domestic Compliance Program (FY2005-FY2018)</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA analytical results table</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2018-lead-in-juice-fy2005-fy2018">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</div>
</details>
